- **m6a-call-reference-overlap-assessment**: Use when per-tool m6A BED call files already exist (upstream tool runs) and the goal is to score each tool against a gold-standard peaks BED at default thresholds (nanom6A probability, yanocomp/differr FDR, drummer P-value, epinano error thresholds) on yeast, mouse or human dRNA-seq data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/m6a-call-reference-overlap-assessment))
- **m6a-colocalization-visualization**: Use when you have RgnTX permutation-test results linking m6A sites to stop codon regions and need to determine whether enrichment is localized at the original region positions or is an artifact, by examining the shifted z-score profile. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/m6a-colocalization-visualization))
- **m6a-deep-learning-model-training**: Use when you have RedNano-extracted comb_basecall_raw features (k-mer, 65-signal window, qual/mis/ins/dele) from modified and unmodified Nanopore DRS FAST5s and need a trained m6A site classifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/m6a-deep-learning-model-training))
- **m6a-detection-output-parsing**: Use when raw outputs from multiple m6A detection tools on Nanopore dRNA-seq data have incompatible score types and formats and must be converted to a common BED format for cross-tool comparison or benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/m6a-detection-output-parsing))
- **m6a-detection-tool-benchmarking**: Use when comparing m6A detection tools on Nanopore direct RNA-seq data, tuning 'default' vs 'relaxed' thresholds, or assessing whether sequencing depth (e.g. 25/50/75% subsampling) saturates m6A hit discovery. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/m6a-detection-tool-benchmarking))

- **m6a-gold-standard-benchmarking**: Use when comparing multiple Nanopore dRNA-seq m6A callers (e.g. 14 tools) on oligos, yeast, mouse, or human datasets against reference m6A peak sets, or when deciding which bin filters or cut-off thresholds improve tool precision/recall. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/m6a-gold-standard-benchmarking))
- **m6a-hit-overlap-computation**: Use when dRNA-seq replicates were analyzed both as separate samples and merged, and you need to quantify per-tool overlap of called m6A bins between the two configurations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/m6a-hit-overlap-computation))

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/m6a-method-benchmarking-against-glori))
- **m6a-methylation-calling-from-current-deviations**: Use when you have ONT fast5/fastq data (basecalled with Albacore or a basecaller retaining event data), a reference assembly, and need per-read and per-position m6A calls at known or suspected methyltransferase motifs. - **m6a-methylation-site-enrichment-testing**: Use when genomic region sets such as miCLIP-seq m6A sites lack isoform annotation and you need a permutation test of their association with transcriptome features (e.g. stop codons) under a transcriptome-wide null model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/m6a-methylation-site-enrichment-testing))
- **m6a-modification-filtering**: Use when nanopore POD5 data has been basecalled (Dorado) and mapped (minimap2) into a modBAM carrying m6A MM/ML tags, and per-position modification calls must be extracted, filtered, and consolidated into genome-wide accessibility intervals. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/m6a-modification-filtering))
- **m6a-motif-enrichment-quantification**: Use when you have CHEUI-solo site-level methylation probabilities from direct RNA sequencing (DRS) and need to compute DRACH motif enrichment and significant m6A site counts at a fixed probability cutoff. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/m6a-motif-enrichment-quantification))
